P4.10.0-C8-I-D4-L-L-O-U — Controlled Activity Intake Auth Context Design Gate
Created: 25 May 2026, Europe/Warsaw

Status
AUTH CONTEXT DESIGN GATE CREATED / DOC ONLY / NO ROUTE CHANGE / NO DATABASE WRITE / NO SQL EXECUTION / NO AI CALL / NO ACTIVITY CREATED

Baseline
- Previous block: P4.10.0-C8-I-D4-L-L-O-T-FIX1
- Previous commit: 4cd975b
- Previous message: docs: verify C8-I-D4-L-L-O-T-FIX1 no-write route

Reason for O-U
O-S created a no-write preview route that temporarily accepts x-controlled-intake-preview-app-user-id.
O-T-FIX1 verified that no-write route behavior.
Before any write route can exist, preview trusted context must be replaced with real authenticated server context.

Gate decision
PASS_AUTH_CONTEXT_RESOLVER_STATIC_HELPER_DESIGN_ONLY

Next step decision
P4.10.0-C8-I-D4-L-L-O-V - Controlled Activity Intake Auth Context Resolver Static Helper

Current route state
- src/app/api/activity/controlled-intake/route.ts exists.
- Route is still no_write_preview only.
- Route still requires x-controlled-intake-no-write-preview: true.
- Route still requires x-controlled-intake-preview-app-user-id.
- Route does not execute DB writes.
- Route does not import Supabase client.
- Route does not call .from().
- Route does not call .insert().
- Route does not call AI.
- Route does not persist semantic candidates, Value Objects, or state layers.

Auth context target
The production trusted context must be built server-side.
The client must not provide:
- user_id
- app_user_id
- owner_id
- actor_id
- organization_id
- space_id
- activity_event_id

Required future resolver behavior
- Read authenticated identity from server-side Auth0/session context.
- Resolve stable app user id from the authenticated identity.
- Optionally resolve actorId / organizationId / spaceId only from server-side data or explicitly selected server-verified context.
- Return null/unauthorized if authenticated user is missing.
- Never accept ownership identifiers from request body.
- Never trust preview headers in production write mode.
- Keep no-write preview route until a separate write proof gate exists.

Recommended future file if gate passes
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/authContext.ts

Recommended future helper exports if gate passes
- CONTROLLED_ACTIVITY_INTAKE_AUTH_CONTEXT_LAYER
- ControlledActivityIntakeAuthContextSource
- ControlledActivityIntakeAuthContextResult
- buildControlledActivityIntakeTrustedContextFromAuth
- buildControlledActivityIntakeNoWritePreviewTrustedContext
- assertControlledActivityIntakeTrustedContext

Important implementation boundary
Even if the next step creates an auth context helper, it must still be no-write.
The route must not execute Supabase inserts.
No database write is approved by O-U.
